# NOVA Document Ownership Registry

**Status:** ACTIVE
**Scope:** documentary stewardship only; no business or technical authority is transferred.

## Assignment policy

- An owner explicitly declared by a source is preserved.
- UNDECLARED owners are assigned by domain through one reusable rule.
- The assignment governs maintenance, review and routing only.

**Documents covered:** 1274  
**Source owners preserved:** 250  
**Owners assigned by SW-003:** 1024  
**Coverage after normalization:** 100%
